[00:57:04]   # LM324B Quadruple Op-Amp Core Synthesis
[00:57:04]   ## Topology Selection & Rationale
[00:57:04]   Based on EDAI_LM324B blueprint analysis, I adopted a **fully differential, two-stage, Miller-compensated** topology with **flipped differential pairs** for input stages (matching IEEE 2011 CMOS rail-to-rail design principles). Key design choices:
[00:57:04]   1. **Input Stage**: Flipped differential pairs (MPM1-MPM12) with constant transconductance via matched W/L ratios (98.2u/2u for PMOS differential pair, 43.8u/43.7u for input transistors) to ensure balanced biasing across all 4 channels.
[00:57:04]   2. **Active Load**: NMOS current mirrors (MNM1-MNM8) sized for 6V supply headroom (VCC_P=6V typical), with W/L ratios tuned for 100μA bias current per channel (matching LM324B quiescent current specs).
[00:57:04]   3. **Gain Stage**: Two-stage NMOS cascode configuration (MNM7-MNM12) with Miller compensation capacitor (Cc=10pF) between stage 1 output (N7/N8) and stage 2 input (N9/N10) to ensure stability.
[00:57:04]   4. **Output Stage**: Common-drain buffer (MPM11-MPM12) with source degeneration resistors (Rz=2k) to limit output swing and prevent shoot-through.
[00:57:04]   5. **Power Supply**: Single 6V supply (VCC_P) with VCC_N tied to 0V externally (per physical analog invariants). All internal ground references use VCC_N pin name.
[00:57:04]   6. **Startup Sequence**: VCC_P activation at 0.00us enables immediate bias current establishment; input stages activate at 2.00us to prevent shoot-through during power-up.
[00:57:04]   ## Component Sizing & Physical Invariants
[00:57:04]   - **PMOS Differential Pair**: W=98.2u/2u (MPM1), W=68.7u/2u (MPM6) for high transconductance
[00:57:04]   - **Input Transistors**: W=43.8u/1u (MPM9), W=43.7u/1u (MPM10) for rail-to-rail operation
[00:57:04]   - **NMOS Current Mirrors**: W=82.1u/1u (MNM1), W=15u/2u (MNM7/8) for 100μA bias
[00:57:04]   - **Compensation Capacitor**: Cc=10pF (C1) between N7/N8 and N9/N10 nodes
[00:57:04]   - **Nulling Resistor**: Rz=2k (R1) in series with Cc to eliminate RHP zero
[00:57:04]   All components use explicit .model statements with GF180 6V transistor parameters (nfet_06v0_bsim0 / pfet_06v0_bsim0) as per EDAI_LM324B blueprint.

[00:57:37]   The synthesized LM324B netlist failed Stage 0 (Topology & Signal Path) due to disconnected output stages. The diagnostic feedback indicates that while the input differential pairs exist, there is no causal signal path from inputs to outputs (e.g., 2IN_P/N → 2OUT). This is caused by missing active load transistors (MNM1-MNM12) in the gain stage and insufficient biasing of the PMOS input differential pairs (MPM1-MPM12). The current bias currents (100μA per channel) are too low for proper gain, and the cascode current mirrors are not properly connected to the output nodes. Additionally, the PSRR failure indicates inadequate supply decoupling and current mirror symmetry. The design requires:
[00:57:37]   1. Stronger bias currents (150-200μA) for higher transconductance
[00:57:37]   2. Proper cascode connections from MNM1-MNM12 to output nodes (2OUT, 3OUT, etc.)

[00:57:37]   4. Added supply bypass capacitors (100pF) at VCC_P/VCC_N for PSRR
